<h2><span>About Us</span></h2>
<p style="text-align:justify">Welcome to AppClick ICT Academy, a subsidiary of AppClick Technology – registered in Nigeria and the United States. Our academy is dedicated to shaping tech leaders of tomorrow, delivering exceptional hands-on training in Software Engineering, Web Design, UI/UX Design, Graphic Design, Digital Marketing, and Digital Literacy.  At AppClick ICT Academy, our mission is to nurture Africa’s Tech leaders and creative visionaries.</p>
<p style="text-align:justify">Our carefully curated courses are designed to empower students with practical experience and relevant skills, enabling them to excel in the dynamic digital landscape. </p>
<p style="text-align:justify">Led by a team of industry experts and passionate instructors, we foster an environment of innovation, collaboration, and growth. Through our comprehensive programs, we aim to inspire and equip individuals from diverse backgrounds to thrive in the ever-evolving Tech industry.</p>

<h3 class="mb-10"><a href="course-details-frontend-development.php" title="Front-end Engineering">Front-end Engineering</a></h3>
<p style="text-align:justify;font-size:1rem;">Front-end engineering focuses on creating the beautiful face of a website or app. It's about designing and building the parts that users see and interact with, like the page structure, buttons, images, and layout. Frontend engineers use languages like HTML, CSS, React Js, Vue js, JavaScript e.tc. to make everything look good and work smoothly. </p>
<p> </p>

<p style="text-align:justify;font-size:1rem;">Back-end engineering involves the development and maintenance of the server-side logic, databases, and systems that power the functionality and data management of applications and websites using technologies like ExpresJS, NodeJS, PHP Laravel, Python, and Django.</p>

<p style="text-align:justify;font-size:1rem;">Full-stack engineering involves becoming skilled in both front-end and back-end development for websites and applications. This means you'll learn how to create the user interface that people see and interact with (front-end) as well as the behind-the-scenes logic, databases, and servers (back-end).</p>

<h3 class="mb-10"><a href="course-details-frontend-development.php" title="Front-end Engineering">Front-end Engineering</a></h3>
<p style="text-align:justify;font-size:1rem;">Front-end engineering focuses on creating the beautiful face of a website or app. It's about designing and building the parts that users see and interact with, like the page structure, buttons, images, and layout. Frontend engineers use languages like HTML, CSS, React Js, Vue js, JavaScript e.tc. to make everything look good and work smoothly. </p>
<p> </p>

<h3 class="mb-10"><a href="course-details-backend-development.php" title="Back-end Engineering">Back-end Engineering</a></h3>
<p style="text-align:justify;font-size:1rem;">Back-end engineering involves the development and maintenance of the server-side logic, databases, and systems that power the functionality and data management of applications and websites using technologies like ExpresJS, NodeJS, PHP Laravel, Python, and Django.</p>
<!--<p style="text-align:justify;">Gain proficiency in languages like PHP (with SQL Database), Node JS(with MongoDB), explore frameworks like Laravel and  Express JS.</p>-->

<!--<span class="course-price"><s style="color:rgb(27, 0, 0);font-weight: 600;">&#8358; 659,000</s> -  &#8358; 395,000</span>-->
<h3 class="mb-10"><a href="course-details-fullstack-development.php" title="Full-stack Engineering">Full-stack Engineering</a></h3>
<p style="text-align:justify;font-size:1rem;">Full-stack engineering involves becoming skilled in both front-end and back-end development for websites and applications. This means you'll learn how to create the user interface that people see and interact with (front-end) as well as the behind-the-scenes logic, databases, and servers (back-end).</p>
